# AGENTS.md — LLM Manager

These instructions apply to AI agents modifying the LLM Manager repository or MARION-IA-USA LLM Manager deployment.

## 1. Read before changing anything

Read, in order:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `FUTURE_WORK.md`
4. `SPRINT.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`
7. `docs/OPERATIONS_AND_USAGE.md`
8. relevant ADRs/TDRs

Approved requirements define scope. Future work is not authorization to implement.

## 2. Service boundary

LLM Manager owns inference/runtime infrastructure concerns. ACMS owns work/project/context orchestration.

Do not move project requirements, Kanban/task state, persistent transcript history, human steering governance, or general ACMS context management into LLM Manager merely because an integration needs them.

## 3. Production safety rules

- Treat MARION-IA-USA as production infrastructure.
- Never replace a known-working model/runtime before capturing its exact rollback configuration.
- Put deployments into explicit maintenance before authorized manual work.
- Recovery has historically had more than one trigger; verify both desired service state and desired power/VM state before stopping a VM.
- Do not assume a stopped model stays stopped until recovery state is verified.
- Never start two VMs that share the same passthrough GPU devices.
- Avoid repeated rapid GPU-VM stop/start cycles on MIAM-00111; a known PCIe/AER/riser condition has previously required a host reboot to recover.
- Preserve custom/patched vLLM environments. Do not `pip install -U vllm` into a known-good runtime.
- Experimental model stacks belong in versioned paths/containers/VMs.

## 4. Configuration and model truth

The live registry and verified runtime state are authoritative for current deployments.

Do not infer that an alias is local merely from its name. Verify provider/runtime/model identity.

When documenting a model deployment, capture:

- actual artifact/revision/checksum;
- runtime/image/commit;
- quantization;
- host/VM/site;
- context;
- TP/PP/DP/EP and relevant runtime flags;
- RAM/VRAM/hardware;
- route/alias;
- health and benchmark evidence.

## 5. History and rollback

Deployment history must make engineering work reproducible.

- `Load Configuration` is non-destructive.
- `Restore & Serve` is a separate confirmed operation.
- Failed experiments may be retained but hidden by default.
- Do not delete the last known-good revision merely because a newer model works.

## 6. Benchmark discipline

A performance number without deployment context is misleading.

Link benchmarks to exact deployment revisions and record at least:

- TTFT;
- output tokens/sec per request/agent;
- aggregate output tokens/sec;
- concurrency;
- prompt/output token counts;
- tested context;
- RAM/VRAM;
- host/hardware/runtime;
- errors;
- representative Hermes/tool-use status when relevant.

When comparing models, prefer same-node tests. When comparing nodes, use the same model/configuration first so hardware effects are visible.

## 7. Secrets and data

Never commit:

- `.env` files containing secrets;
- API keys/tokens;
- passwords;
- SSH/private keys;
- production database row dumps;
- customer-sensitive data;
- raw logs containing credentials.

Sanitize configuration into `.example` files. Run a secret scan before commit. If a secret appears in a tool transcript or repository candidate, stop and surface it for rotation/removal.

## 8. Git workflow

- Work on a dedicated branch.
- Branch format: `type/ticket-short-description`.
- Commit format: `type(scope): short description (#issue-id)`.
- Do not invent an issue ID; create/use an approved issue according to repository policy.
- Keep capture/bootstrapping changes reviewable.
- A human must approve before merge.

## 9. ADR policy

If a human explicitly made a significant architecture decision, record it as an ADR and surface it in the PR.

If the agent thinks a significant new decision is required, draft `Status: Proposed` and stop for human approval before treating it as accepted.

## 10. TDR policy

Use a TDR for intentional shortcuts, patched runtimes, manual recovery procedures, missing automation, known hardware/software limitations, or other accepted debt.

## 11. Validation

Run the exact project validation commands once captured/documented. At minimum for a Python service, consider syntax/import checks, tests, config parsing, migration/schema verification, and API smoke tests in a safe environment.

Report `Pass`, `Fail`, or `Not run` accurately. Never infer a passing test.

## 12. Stop and ask for human judgment when

- requirements conflict;
- an ACMS-facing contract would be invented or materially changed;
- a destructive production action is required outside the approved maintenance scope;
- credentials/access are required and unavailable;
- a security boundary changes;
- production tests fail unexpectedly;
- a new persistent model policy would be made without approved authority;
- the diff expands beyond the approved capture/documentation scope.

## 13. Handoff standard

At completion provide:

1. requirements addressed;
2. source/code captured and source locations;
3. files excluded and why;
4. changes made;
5. validation commands/results;
6. production impact (if any);
7. ADR/TDR status;
8. future-work items discovered;
9. known risks/unresolved questions;
10. exact branch, commit, PR, and recommended next action.

# AGENTS.md

Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous coding agents working in this repository.

## 1. Authority model

Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, and approve merges.

Agents may:

- inspect the repository;
- propose a short plan;
- implement approved requirements;
- create or update automated tests;
- update documentation affected by the change;
- add newly discovered ideas to `FUTURE_WORK.md`;
- draft ADRs and TDRs;
- run non-destructive validation;
- prepare commits and pull requests.

Agents must not:

- silently add product scope;
- promote future work into approved requirements without human approval;
- accept their own newly invented significant architecture decision;
- merge their own work under the current operating model;
- commit directly to `main`/production branches;
- use destructive commands, rotate secrets, alter production data, change permissions, or modify infrastructure without explicit human approval;
- hide failed validation, security concerns, or unresolved trade-offs.

## 2. Read before planning

For every meaningful change, read the relevant parts of:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `SPRINT.md`
4. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
5. relevant files in `docs/adr/`
6. relevant files in `docs/tdr/`
7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope or follow-up work is involved

Repository-local instructions and approved requirements are the primary source of truth.

## 3. Requirement discipline

Implementation work should trace to one or more `REQ-###` entries.

Before editing, summarize:

- the requirement IDs being implemented;
- the acceptance criteria;
- the files/components likely to change;
- assumptions or ambiguities;
- the validation that will prove the change works.

If the requested work is not covered by an approved requirement, do one of the following:

- ask for human approval to add/modify the requirement; or
- record the idea in `FUTURE_WORK.md` if it is not part of the current scope.

Do not invent scope merely because it seems useful.

## 4. Planning and execution

Before meaningful edits, provide a short implementation plan. Keep the plan proportional to the change.

During execution:

- work in a dedicated branch/worktree/sandbox;
- keep the diff focused;
- follow the existing architecture and project conventions;
- prefer readable, conventional code over clever code;
- avoid unrelated cleanup;
- preserve backward compatibility unless an approved requirement or ADR says otherwise;
- update affected documentation in the same change.

If the implementation grows materially beyond the approved plan, stop and surface the scope increase.

## 5. Significant decisions and ADRs

Create or update an ADR when a change makes a significant, durable decision about architecture, major dependencies, data/storage, external integrations, security boundaries, deployment model, scalability approach, or another choice that future maintainers are likely to ask "why did we choose this?"

### Human-directed decision

If a human explicitly made the decision, the agent may record it as `Status: Accepted`. The ADR must still be called out in the pull request so the human sees what was recorded.

### Agent-originated decision

If the agent concludes a significant decision is needed, create a draft ADR with `Status: Proposed` and surface it for human approval. Do not treat it as approved until a human accepts it.

Use `docs/adr/TEMPLATE.md`.

## 6. Technical debt and TDRs

Create a TDR when a change intentionally leaves known technical debt such as a shortcut, workaround, temporary design, missing test, manual step, or deferred reliability/security/performance/maintainability improvement.

Use `docs/tdr/TEMPLATE.md` and keep the record concise.

An agent may propose a TDR, but the human reviewer should confirm that the debt is acceptable and that the owner is appropriate.

## 7. Validation

Use the exact validation commands documented by the project once the implementation stack is selected.

Run all checks relevant to the change, such as:

- formatting;
- lint/static analysis;
- type checking where applicable;
- unit tests;
- integration tests;
- end-to-end tests where applicable;
- build/package validation;
- security/secret/dependency checks;
- deployment or readiness checks when relevant.

Report exactly what passed, failed, or was not run. Never imply that a check passed if it was skipped or unavailable.

If expected commands are not documented, surface that as an agent-readiness gap instead of guessing silently.

## 8. Security and data handling

- Do not commit secrets, credentials, private keys, access tokens, or sensitive customer/company data.
- Treat generated shell commands, migrations, dependencies, and configuration changes as untrusted until reviewed.
- Do not weaken authentication, authorization, encryption, logging, isolation, or monitoring merely to make a task easier.
- Surface security-sensitive behavior for explicit human review.

## 9. Git and pull requests

Startup Teams Git policy applies unless a stricter project policy is documented.

- Use a dedicated branch; direct commits to `main`/production branches are prohibited.
- Branch format: `type/ticket-short-description` (for example, `feat/AGR-24-bnpl-checkout`).
- Commit format: `type(scope): short description (#issue-id)`.
- Commit scope is required.
- Every commit must reference the related issue/ticket.
- Keep commits and PRs small enough to review.
- Required validation must pass before merge.
- A human reviewer must approve before merge under the current operating model.

Every PR should state:

- feature / requirement IDs;
- what changed and why;
- files/components changed;
- validation performed and results;
- known risks or limitations;
- documentation changes;
- ADRs/TDRs added or changed;
- future-work items discovered;
- reviewer focus areas.

## 10. Stop and ask for human judgment when

- requirements conflict or are materially ambiguous;
- a new significant ADR decision is required;
- credentials or restricted access are required;
- a destructive action is needed;
- tests fail unexpectedly and the cause is not understood;
- security-sensitive behavior changes;
- production data or infrastructure would be changed;
- the diff grows substantially beyond the planned scope;
- a future-work idea would need to become approved product scope.

## 11. Handoff standard

At the end of a work session or before handing work to another agent/human, provide:

1. requirement IDs addressed;
2. concise summary of changes;
3. files changed;
4. validation commands and results;
5. known risks / unresolved questions;
6. ADR/TDR status;
7. future-work items discovered;
8. recommended next action.

## 12. PDU Manager production safety additions

This application can control real electrical power. Treat production interactions as high-consequence infrastructure operations.

Agents MUST NOT perform real PDU ON/OFF/REBOOT/CYCLE operations as tests unless a human explicitly authorizes the exact outlet/action for that session.

During normal development, source capture, CI, staging, or deployment verification:

- use mocks/fixtures for actuation behavior;
- prefer read-only outlet-state checks when technically enforceable;
- do not reboot VM154 or `MIAM-00133` merely to apply application changes;
- restart only the application service(s) when a service restart is sufficient;
- do not modify production outlet labels, presets, protection/lockout settings, or credentials as an incidental test;
- never place PDU/LDAP/TLS credentials in Git;
- preserve protection of critical power targets;
- treat any change that could remove a safety interlock as security/safety sensitive and stop for human review.

The production endpoint is `https://10.0.20.156/` on VM156 hosted by `MIAM-00133` (post-cutover 2026-09-27; VM154 is powered-off fallback). This location is an approved deployment target, not permission for unrestricted infrastructure changes.

## 13. Har

Runs ONLY on the dedicated\n# MARION self-hosted runner (never GitHub-hosted; never runs PR code).\n# Trigger: successful ci.yml `artifact` job on main.\n\non:\n  workflow_run:\n    workflows: [\"ci\"]\n    types: [completed]\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n  actions: read\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: staging-deploy\n  cancel-in-progress: false\n\njobs:\n  deploy-staging:\n    if: >-\n      github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' ||\n      (github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'success' &&\n       github.event.workflow_run.head_branch == 'main')\n    runs-on: [self-hosted, marion, llm-manager-deploy]\n    steps:\n      - name: Resolve CI artifact SHA\n        id: sha\n        run: echo \"sha=$(git -C /opt/llm-manager-deploy/workspace rev-parse origin/main 2>/dev/null || echo '')\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n\n      - name: Download EXACT CI artifact (same SHA that passed CI — never rebuilt)\n        uses: actions/github-script@v7\n        with:\n          script: |\n            const run = context.payload.workflow_run;\n            const artifacts = await github.rest.actions.listWorkflowRunArtifacts({\n              owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo,\n              run_id: run.id,\n            });\n            const art = artifacts.data.artifacts.find(a => a.name.startsWith(\"llm-manager-\"));\n            if (!art) throw new Error(\"no llm-manager artifact on CI run\");\n            const dl = await github.rest.actions.downloadArtifact({\n              owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo,\n              artifact_id: art.id, archive_format: \"zip\",\n            });\n            const fs = require(\"fs\");\n            fs.writeFileSync(`${process.env.RUNNER_TEMP}/artifact.zip`, Buffer.from(dl.data));\n\n      - name: Unpack + checksum-verify artifact\n        run: |\n          cd \"$RUNNER_TEMP\"\n          unzip -o artifact.zip\n          ART=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -name 'llm-manager-*.tar.gz' | head -1)\n          [ -n \"$ART\" ] || { echo \"artifact missing\"; exit 1; }\n          sha256sum -c \"${ART}.sha256\"\n          echo \"ARTIFACT=$ART\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"\n\n      - name: Preflight staging\n        run: ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131 \"bash -s\" < deploy/preflight.sh \"$ARTIFACT\" --staging || true\n\n      - name: Transfer artifact to staging\n        run: scp \"$ARTIFACT\" \"$ARTIFACT.sha256\" llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131:/tmp/\n\n      - name: Bootstrap (first deploy) or release-transaction deploy\n        run: |\n          ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131 \"bash -s\" <<'REMOTE'\n          set -euo pipefail\n          ART=$(ls /tmp/llm-manager-*.tar.gz | head -1)\n          if [ ! -e /opt/llm-manager/current ]; then\n            sudo /opt/llm-manager-deploy/bootstrap-wrapper.sh \"$ART\" --staging\n          else\n            sudo /opt/llm-manager-deploy/deploy-wrapper.sh \"$ART\" --staging\n          fi\n          REMOTE\n\n      - name: Post-deploy acceptance (healthcheck)\n        run: ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131 \"sudo /opt/llm-manager-deploy/healthcheck-wrapper.sh --staging\"\n\n      - name: Record deployed SHA\n        run: |\n          ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131 \"cat /opt/llm-manager/current/release_manifest.json | python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(sys.stdin)[\\\"git_sha\\\"])'\" > deployed-sha.txt\n          echo \"staging deployed at $(cat deployed-sha.txt)\"\n=== deploy-production.yml ===\nname: deploy-production\n\n# §12: production CD — workflow_dispatch ONLY initially. GitHub Environment\n# `production` requires a human reviewer. Deploys the SAME artifact/SHA that\n# passed staging — the runner downloads the artifact, never rebuilds.\n\non:\n  workflow_dispatch:\n    inputs:\n      git_sha:\n        description: \"Release git SHA to promote (must exist as an artifact)\"\n        required: true\n        type: string\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n  actions: read\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: production-deploy\n  cancel-in-progress: false\n\njobs:\n  deploy-production:\n    environment: production\n    runs-on: [self-hosted, marion, llm-manager-deploy]\n    steps:\n      - name: Locate artifact for requested SHA\n        uses: actions/github-script@v7\n        with:\n          script: |\n            const sha = context.payload.inputs.git_sha;\n            const runs = await github.paginate(github.rest.actions.listWorkflowRuns, {\n              owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo,\n              workflow_id: \"ci.yml\", status: \"success\", head_branch: \"main\",\n              per_page: 100,\n            });\n            for (const run of runs) {\n              if (run.head_sha.startsWith(sha)) {\n                const arts = await github.rest.actions.listWorkflowRunArtifacts({\n                  owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo, run_id: run.id,\n                });\n                const art = arts.data.artifacts.find(a => a.name.startsWith(\"llm-manager-\"));\n                if (art) {\n                  const dl = await github.rest.actions.downloadArtifact({\n                    owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo,\n                    artifact_id: art.id, archive_format: \"zip\",\n                  });\n                  const fs = require(\"fs\");\n                  fs.writeFileSync(`${process.env.RUNNER_TEMP}/artifact.zip`, Buffer.from(dl.data));\n                  console.log(`using artifact from run ${run.id} (${run.head_sha})`);\n                  return;\n                }\n              }\n            }\n            throw new Error(`no successful CI artifact found for sha ${sha}`);\n\n      - name: Verify checksum\n        run: |\n          cd \"$RUNNER_TEMP\"\n          unzip -o artifact.zip\n          ART=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -name 'llm-manager-*.tar.gz' | head -1)\n          sha256sum -c \"${ART}.sha256\"\n          MAN_SHA=$(tar -xzOf \"$ART\" --wildcards '*/release_manifest.json' | python3 -c 'import json,sys;print(json.load(sys.stdin)[\"git_sha\"])')\n          echo \"artifact git_sha: $MAN_SHA\"\n          case \"$MAN_SHA\" in \"${{ inputs.git_sha }}\"*) ;; *) echo \"SHA MISMATCH\"; exit 1;; esac\n          echo \"ARTIFACT=$ART\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"\n\n      - name: Transfer to VM114\n        run: scp \"$ARTIFACT\" \"$ARTIFACT.sha256\" llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.108:/tmp/\n\n      - name: Production release transaction (preflight → install → validate →\n          migrate → activate → recovery-last restart → smoke → auto-rollback)\n        run: |\n          ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.108 \"bash -s\" <<'REMOTE'\n          set -euo pipefail\n          ART=$(ls /tmp/llm-manager-*.tar.gz | head -1)\n          sudo /opt/llm-manager-deploy/deploy-wrapper.sh \"$ART\"\n          REMOTE\n\n      - name: Post-deploy acceptance\n        run: ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.108 \"sudo /opt/llm-manager-deploy/healthcheck-wrapper.sh\"", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"content": "1|name: deploy-staging\n2|\n3|# §11: staging CD — automatic after merge to main. Runs ONLY on the dedicated\n4|# MARION self-hosted runner (never GitHub-hosted; never runs PR code).\n5|# Trigger: successful ci.yml `artifact` job on main.\n6|\n7|on:\n8|  workflow_run:\n9|    workflows: [\"ci\"]\n10|    types: [completed]\n11|  workflow_dispatch:\n12|\n13|permissions:\n14|  contents: read\n15|  actions: read\n16|\n17|concurrency:\n18|  group: staging-deploy\n19|  cancel-in-progress: false\n20|\n21|jobs:\n22|  deploy-staging:\n23|    if: >-\n24|      github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' ||\n25|      (github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'success' &&\n26|       github.event.workflow_run.head_branch == 'main')\n27|    runs-on: [self-hosted, marion, llm-manager-deploy]\n28|    steps:\n29|      - name: Resolve CI artifact SHA\n30|        id: sha\n31|        run: echo \"sha=$(git -C /opt/llm-manager-deploy/workspace rev-parse origin/main 2>/dev/null || echo '')\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n32|\n33|      - name: Download EXACT CI artifact (same SHA that passed CI — never rebuilt)\n34|        uses: actions/github-script@v7\n35|        with:\n36|          script: |\n37|            const run = context.payload.workflow_run;\n38|            const artifacts = await github.rest.actions.listWorkflowRunArtifacts({\n39|              owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo,\n40|              run_id: run.id,\n41|            });\n42|            const art = artifacts.data.artifacts.find(a => a.name.startsWith(\"llm-manager-\"));\n43|            if (!art) throw new Error(\"no llm-manager artifact on CI run\");\n44|            const dl = await github.rest.actions.downloadArtifact({\n45|              owner: context.repo.owner, repo: context.repo.repo,\n46|              artifact_id: art.id, archive_format: \"zip\",\n47|            });\n48|            const fs = require(\"fs\");\n49|            fs.writeFileSync(`${process.env.RUNNER_TEMP}/artifact.zip`, Buffer.from(dl.data));\n50|\n51|      - name: Unpack + checksum-verify artifact\n52|        run: |\n53|          cd \"$RUNNER_TEMP\"\n54|          unzip -o artifact.zip\n55|          ART=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -name 'llm-manager-*.tar.gz' | head -1)\n56|          [ -n \"$ART\" ] || { echo \"artifact missing\"; exit 1; }\n57|          sha256sum -c \"${ART}.sha256\"\n58|          echo \"ARTIFACT=$ART\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"\n59|\n60|      - name: Preflight staging\n61|        run: ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131 \"bash -s\" < deploy/preflight.sh \"$ARTIFACT\" --staging || true\n62|\n63|      - name: Transfer artifact to staging\n64|        run: scp \"$ARTIFACT\" \"$ARTIFACT.sha256\" llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131:/tmp/\n65|\n66|      - name: Bootstrap (first deploy) or release-transaction deploy\n67|        run: |\n68|          ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131 \"bash -s\" <<'REMOTE'\n69|          set -euo pipefail\n70|          ART=$(ls /tmp/llm-manager-*.tar.gz | head -1)\n71|          if [ ! -e /opt/llm-manager/current ]; then\n72|            sudo /opt/llm-manager-deploy/bootstrap-wrapper.sh \"$ART\" --staging\n73|          else\n74|            sudo /opt/llm-manager-deploy/deploy-wrapper.sh \"$ART\" --staging\n75|          fi\n76|          REMOTE\n77|\n78|      - name: Post-deploy acceptance (healthcheck)\n79|        run: ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131 \"sudo /opt/llm-manager-deploy/healthcheck-wrapper.sh --staging\"\n80|\n81|      - name: Record deployed SHA\n82|        run: |\n83|          ssh llm-manager-deploy@10.0.20.131 \"cat /opt/llm-manager/current/release_manifest.json | python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(sys.stdin)[\\\"git_sha\\\"])'\" > deployed-sha.txt\n84|          echo \"staging deployed at $(cat deployed-sha.txt)\"\n85|", "total_lines": 84, "file_size": 3377, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

then\n    ok \"artifact checksum\"\nelse fail \"artifact checksum\"; fi\n\n# release identity\nREL_SHA=\"$(tar -xzOf \"$TARBALL\" --wildcards '*/release_manifest.json' 2>/dev/null | python3 -c 'import json,sys;print(json.load(sys.stdin)[\"git_sha\"])' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n[ -n \"$REL_SHA\" ] && ok \"release manifest (git_sha=${REL_SHA:0:12})\" || fail \"release manifest unreadable\"\n\n# current release record\nif [ -e \"$APP_ROOT/current\" ]; then\n    ok \"current release: $(readlink \"$APP_ROOT/current\" | xargs basename 2>/dev/null || readlink \"$APP_ROOT/current\")\"\nelse\n    [ \"$STAGING\" = \"1\" ] && echo \"  INFO: no current release yet (fresh staging)\" || fail \"no current release symlink\"\nfi\n\n# disk space (need >= 2 GiB free on /)\nAVAIL=$(df --output=avail -BG / | tail -1 | tr -dc '0-9')\n[ \"$AVAIL\" -ge 2 ] && ok \"disk space (${AVAIL}G free)\" || fail \"disk space (${AVAIL}G free < 2G)\"\n\n# python runtime compatibility\nPYVER=\"$(python3 -c 'import sys; print(f\"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}\")' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n[ \"$PYVER\" = \"3.12\" ] && ok \"python ${PYVER}\" || fail \"python 3.12 required, found '${PYVER:-none}'\"\n\n# external files: secrets dir present (names only check)\nif [ -d /etc/llm-manager/secrets ] && [ -n \"$(ls -A /etc/llm-manager/secrets 2>/dev/null)\" ]; then\n=== preflight args expectation ===\n10:ok()   { echo \"  PASS: $1\"; }\n11:fail() { echo \"  FAIL: $1\"; FAIL=1; }", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

shift || true\n10|STAGING=0; SKIP_MIG=0\n11|for a in \"$@\"; do\n12|  [ \"$a\" = \"--staging\" ] && STAGING=1\n13|  [ \"$a\" = \"--skip-migrations\" ] && SKIP_MIG=1\n14|done\n15|\n16|APP_ROOT=/opt/llm-manager\n17|RELEASES=$APP_ROOT/releases\n18|LEDGER=/var/log/llm-manager/release-ledger.jsonl\n19|mkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LEDGER\")\" \"$APP_ROOT/shared/logs\" \"$APP_ROOT/shared/state\"\n20|\n21|stamp() { date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ; }\n22|ledger() { echo \"{\\\"ts\\\":\\\"$(stamp)\\\",\\\"event\\\":\\\"$1\\\",\\\"detail\\\":$2}\" >> \"$LEDGER\"; }\n23|\n24|die() { echo \"FATAL: $*\" >&2; ledger \"abort\" \"\\\"$*\\\"\"; exit 1; }\n25|\n26|echo \"==> LLM Manager release transaction ($(stamp))\"\n27|\n28|# ---- Phase A: preflight --------------------------------------------------------\n29|\"$(dirname \"$0\")/preflight.sh\" \"$TARBALL\" $([ \"$STAGING\" = 1 ] && echo --staging) || die \"preflight failed\"\n30|\n31|# snapshot current state\n32|PREV_REL=\"\"\n33|[ -e \"$APP_ROOT/current\" ] && PREV_REL=\"$(basename \"$(readlink -f \"$APP_ROOT/current\")\")\"\n34|PREV_HEALTH=\"$(curl -sk --max-time 5 http://127.0.0.1/healthz 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n35|ledger \"snapshot\" \"{\\\"previous_release\\\":\\\"$PREV_REL\\\",\\\"health\\\":$(echo \"${PREV_HEALTH:-null}\" | python3 -c 'import sys,json; s=sys.stdin.read().strip(); print(s if s.startswith(\"{\") else \"null\")')}\"\n36|echo \"==> snapshot: previous release ${PREV_REL:-none}\"\n37|\n38|# ---- Phase B: unpack + verify artifact -----------------------------------------\n39|STAGE=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\n40|tar -xzf \"$TARBALL\" -C \"$STAGE\"\n41|REL_SRC=\"$(find \"$STAGE\" -maxdepth 1 -type d -name 'llm-manager-*' | head -1)\"\n42|[ -n \"$REL_SRC\" ] || die \"unexpected artifact layout\"\n43|REL_SHA=\"$(python3 -c \"import json;print(json.load(open('$REL_SRC/release_manifest.json'))['git_sha'])\")\"\n44|REL_DST=\"$RELEASES/$REL_SHA\"\n45|\n46|# verify manifest checksums of what we unpacked\n47|python3 - \"$REL_SRC\" <<'PY' || die \"artifact manifest verification failed\"\n48|import hashlib, json, os, sys\n49|stage = sys.argv[1]\n50|man = json.load(open(os.path.join(stage, \"release_manifest.json\")))\n51|bad = []\n52|for rel, expect in man[\"files\"].items():\n53|    p = os.path.join(stage, rel)\n54|    if not os.path.exists(p) or hashlib.sha256(open(p, \"rb\").read()).hexdigest() != expect:\n55|        bad.append(rel)\n56|if bad:\n57|    print(\"corrupt artifact files:\", bad[:5]); sys.exit(1)\n58|print(f\"manifest verified: {len(man['files'])} files\")\n59|PY\n60|\n61|# ---- Phase C: suppress interference (recovery FIRST to stop) --------------------\n62|if systemctl is-active llm-manager-recovery >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n63|    systemctl stop llm-manager-recovery && echo \"==> recovery stopped (suppressed for deploy)\"\n64|    ledger \"recovery_stopped\" \"null\"\n65|fi\n66|# NOTE: hosted model servers are NEVER touched by a manager deploy (plan §13).\n67|\n68|# ---- Phase D: install candidate --------------------------------------------------\n69|if [ -d \"$REL_DST\" ]; then\n70|    echo \"==> candidate $REL_SHA already present (re-release of same SHA)\"\n71|else\n72|    mkdir -p \"$REL_DST\"\n73|    cp -a \"$REL_SRC\"/. \"$REL_DST\"/\n74|    echo \"==> candidate installed at $REL_DST\"\n75|fi\n76|rm -rf \"$STAGE\"\n77|\n78|# release venv (idempotent)\n79|VENV=\"$REL_DST/.venv\"\n80|if [ ! -x \"$VENV/bin/python\" ]; then\n81|    python3 -m venv \"$VENV\"\n82|    \"$VENV/bin/pip\" install --quiet --upgrade pip\n83|    \"$VENV/bin/pip\" install --quiet -r \"$REL_DST/config/examples/requirements-v011-venv-freeze.txt\"\n84|fi\n85|for d in logs state; do [ -e \"$REL_DST/$d\" ] || ln -s \"$APP_ROOT/shared/$d\" \"$REL_DST/$d\"; done\n86|\n87|# legacy path compat symlinks (same as bootstrap §5b)\n88|ln -sfn \"$REL_DST/service/app\"          \"$REL_DST/app\"\n89|ln -sfn \"$REL_DST/service/collectors\"   \"$REL_DST/collectors\"\n90|ln -sfn \"$REL_DST/service/control\"      \"$REL_DST/control\"\n91|ln -sfn \"$REL_DST/service/agentmanager\" \"$REL_DST/agentmanager\"\n92|ln -sfn \"$REL_DST/.venv\"                \"$REL_DST/venv\"\n93|\n94|# install/refresh unit files pointing at current/\n95|for unit in \"$REL_DST\"/ops/systemd/*.service; do\n96|    sed -e 's#/opt/llm-manager/app#/opt/llm-manager/current/app#g' \\\n97|        -e 's#/opt/llm-manager/venv#/opt/llm-manager/current/venv#g' \\\n98|        -e 's#/opt/llm-manager/collectors#/opt/llm-manager/current/collectors#g' \\\n99|        \"$unit\" > \"/etc/systemd/system/$(basename \"$unit\")\"\n100|done\n101|systemctl daemon-reload\n102|\n103|# ---- Phase E: candidate validation (before activation) ----------------------------\n104|echo \"==> candidate validation\"\n105|\"$VENV/bin/python\" -m py_compile \"$REL_DST\"/service/app/*.py \"$REL_DST\"/service/collectors/*.py \"$REL_DST\"/service/control/*.py \\\n106|    || die \"candidate py_compile failed\"\n107|\"$VENV/bin/python\" \"$REL_DST/service/app/v011_cost_tests.py\" >/dev/null || die \"candidate cost tests failed\"\n108|\"$VENV/bin/python\" -c \"import sys; {"status": "success", "output": "\r<ger:~$ sudo cat /opt/llm-manager-deploy/rollback.sh\r\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\r\n# rollback.sh — STANDALONE application-only rollback to a previous release (plan §14).\r\n# Usage: rollback.sh [<target-release-sha>]\r\n#\r\n# Policy: application-only. This script NEVER touches the database — DB changes are\r\n# forward-only via migrations (destructive migrations are blocked from ordinary CD by\r\n# policy), so application rollback is safe alongside expand-only schema history.\r\n# Recovery is restarted LAST.\r\nset -uo pipefail\r\nAPP_ROOT=/opt/llm-manager\r\nRELEASES=$APP_ROOT/releases\r\nLEDGER=/var/log/llm-manager/release-ledger.jsonl\r\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LEDGER\")\"\r\n\r\nCURRENT=\"$(basename \"$(readlink -f \"$APP_ROOT/current\" 2>/dev/null)\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\r\n\r\nif [ -n \"${1:-}\" ]; then TARGET=\"$1\"; else\r\n    # previous = most recent release dir that is not current\r\n    TARGET=\"$(ls -1t \"$RELEASES\" 2>/dev/null | grep -v \"^$CURRENT$\" | head -1 || true)\"\r\nfi\r\n[ -n \"$TARGET\" ] && [ -d \"$RELEASES/$TARGET\" ] || { echo \"FATAL: no rollback target found\"; exit 1; }\r\n[ \"$TARGET\" != \"$CURRENT\" ] || { echo \"FATAL: target == current ($CURRENT)\"; exit 1; }\r\n\r\necho \"==> rolling back $CURRENT -> $TARGET\"\r\necho '{\"ts\":\"'\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"'\",\"event\":\"manual_rollback_start\",\"detail\":{\"from\":\"'\"$CURRENT\"'\",\"to\":\"'\"$TARGET\"'\"}}' >> \"$LEDGER\"\r\n\r\nsystemctl stop llm-manager-recovery || true   # suppress interference first\r\n\r\nln -sfn \"$RELEASES/$TARGET\" \"$APP_ROOT/current.new\"\r\nmv -T \"$APP_ROOT/current.new\" \"$APP_ROOT/current\"\r\nsystemctl daemon-reload\r\n\r\nsystemctl restart llm-manager-web      || true\r\nsystemctl restart llm-manager-collector || true\r\nsystemctl restart llm-manager-emporia  || true\r\nsystemctl restart llm-manager-recovery || true   # LAST\r\n\r\nsleep 3\r\nif \"$(dirname \"$0\")/healthcheck.sh\"; then\r\n    echo \"==> rollback COMPLETE ($TARGET active)\"\r\n    echo '{\"ts\":\"'\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"'\",\"event\":\"manual_rollback_complete\",\"detail\":{\"release\":\"'\"$TARGET\"'\"}}' >> \"$LEDGER\"\r\n    exit 0\r\nfi\r\necho \"==> rollback healthcheck FAILED — degraded; human intervention required\"\r\necho '{\"ts\":\"'\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"'\",\"event\":\"manual_rollback_degraded\",\"detail\":{\"release\":\"'\"$TARGET\"'\"}}' >> \"$LEDGER\"\r\nexit 1\r\nubuntu@marion-ia-usa-llm-manager:~\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.58}

{"status": "success", "output": "def next_vmid():\n    return int(pve(\"/cluster/nextid\")[\"data\"])\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ provisioning engine (§16 state machine)\ndef _step(cur, job_id, seq, step, state=\"DONE\", detail=None):\n    cur.execute(\"\"\"INSERT INTO provisioning_steps (job_id, seq, step, state, detail)\n                   VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)\n                   ON CONFLICT (job_id, seq) DO UPDATE SET step=EXCLUDED.step,\n                   state=EXCLUDED.state, detail=EXCLUDED.detail\"\"\",\n                (job_id, seq, step, state, detail))\n    cur.execute(\"UPDATE provisioning_jobs SET state=%s, step=%s WHERE job_id=%s\",\n                (state if state != \"DONE\" else \"RUNNING\", step, job_id))\n\n\ndef provision_job(job_id, agent_id, name):\n    conn = pg()\n    try:\n        cur = conn.cursor()\n        cur.execute(\"SELECT state FROM provisioning_jobs WHERE job_id=%s\", (job_id,))\n        if not cur.fetchone():\n            conn.close(); return\n        _step(cur, job_id, 1, \"VALIDATING\", \"RUNNING\")\n        cur.execute(\"SELECT name FROM agents WHERE agent_id=%s\", (agent_id,))\n        conn.commit()\n\n        vmid = next_vmid()  # avoid racing other allocators\n        _step(cur, job_id, 2, \"RESERVING\", \"DONE\")\n        _step(cur, job_id, 3, \"CLONING_VM\", \"RUNNING\")\n        cur.execute(\"UPDATE provisioning_jobs SET vmid=%s WHERE job_id=%s\", (vmid, job_id))\n        conn.commit()\n        pve(f\"/nodes/{TEMPLATE_NODE}/qemu/{TEMPLATE_VMID}/clone\", method=\"POST\",\n            body={\"newid\": vmid, \"name\": name[:63], \"full\": 1, \"target\": TEMPLATE_NODE})\n        # wait for clone lock release\n        for _ in range(120):\n            try:\n                cfg = (pve(f\"/nodes/{TEMPLATE_NODE}/qemu/{vmid}/config\") or {}).get(\"data\") or {}\n            except Exception:\n                time.sleep(2)\n                continue\n            if not cfg.get(\"lock\"):\n                break\n            time.sleep(2)\n        _step(cur, job_id, 3, \"DONE\")\n\n        _step(cur, job_id, 4, \"CONFIGURING_CLOUD_INIT\", \"RUNNING\")\n        conn.commit()\n        pve(f\"/nodes/{TEMPLATE_NODE}/qemu/{vmid}/config\", method=\"POST\",\n            body={\"net0\": f\"virtio,bridge={BRIDGE}\", \"ipconfig0\": \"ip=dhcp\", \"ciuser\": \"stagent\"})\n        _step(cur, job_id, 4, \"DONE\")\n\n        _step(cur, job_id, 5, \"BOOTING\", \"RUNNING\")\n        pve(f\"/nodes/{TEMPLATE_NODE}/qemu/{vmid}/status/start\", method=\"POST\")\n        conn.commit()\n\n        ip = None\n        for _ in range(90):\n            try:\n                ifs = pve(f\"/nodes/{TEMPLATE_NODE}/qemu/{vmid}/agent/network-get-interfaces\")\n                for ifc in (ifs.get(\"data\", {}).get(\"result\") or []):\n                    for a in (ifc.get(\"ip-addresses\") or []):\n                        ipaddr = a.get(\"ip-address\", \"\")\n                        if a.get(\"ip-address-type\") == \"ipv4\" and not ipaddr.startswith(\"127.\"):\n                            ip = ipaddr\n                if ip:\n                    break\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            time.sleep(3)\n        if not ip:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"no IP after boot (270s)\")\n        _step(cur, job_id, 5, \"DONE\")\n\n        _step(cur, job_id, 6, \"WAITING_GUEST\", \"DONE\")\n        _step(cur, job_id, 7, \"INJECTING_AGENT_CONFIG\", \"RUNNING\")\n        # per-agent token, replace template placeholder token\n        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(24)\n        out, err, rc = pve_exec(TEMPLATE_NODE, vmid, [\n            \"bash\", \"-c\",\n            f\"mkdir -p /etc/st-agentd && echo '{token}' > /etc/st-agentd/token && \"\n            f\"chmod 600 /etc/st-agentd/token && systemctl enable --now st-agentd && \"\n            f\"systemctl restart st-agentd\"])\n        if rc != 0:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"agentd inject failed: {err[:200]}\")\n        _step(cur, job_id, 7, \"DONE\")\n\n        # ---- step 7.5: per-agent LLM key (LiteLLM virtual key, alias = agent name)\n        _step(cur, job_id, 75, \"PROVISIONING_LLM_KEY\", \"RUNNING\")\n        master = sec(\"litellm_master_key\")\n        if not master:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"litellm_master_key secret missing — cannot provision LLM key\")\n        key_resp = requests.post(f\"{LITELLM_URL}/key/generate\",\n            headers={\"Authorizat\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}

dead-VM agents -> ERROR\n    try:\n        pve_vms = set()\n        r = pve(\"/cluster/resources?type=vm\")\n        for g in (r.get(\"data\") or []):\n            pve_vms.add(g.get(\"vmid\"))\n        fixes = []\n        for i, r_ in enumerate(rows):\n            st, vmid = r_[5], r_[6]\n            if st in (\"READY\", \"STOPPED\", \"PROVISIONING\") and vmid and vmid not in pve_vms:\n                fixes.append(r_[0])\n        if fixes:\n            conn = pg(); cur = conn.cursor()\n            for aid in fixes:\n                cur.execute(\"\"\"UPDATE agents SET state='ERROR', updated_at=NOW()\n                               WHERE agent_id=%s\"\"\", (aid,))\n                audit(\"system\", \"agent_state_reconciled\", str(aid), \"ok\",\n                      {\"reason\": \"vm missing in PVE\"})\n            conn.commit(); conn.close()\n            # re-read rows so the response reflects reality\n            conn = pg(); cur = conn.cursor()\n            if user == \"root\" or role == \"Security-Admin\":\n                cur.execute(\"\"\"SELECT agent_id, name, agent_type, owner_username, harness, state,\n                    vmid, node, agent_ip, model_profile, created_at\n                    FROM agents ORDER BY created_at DESC\"\"\")\n            else:\n                cur.execute(\"\"\"SELECT DISTINCT a.agent_id, a.name, a.agent_type, a.owner_username, a.harness,\n                    a.state, a.vmid, a.node, a.agent_ip, a.model_profile, a.created_at\n                    FROM agents a LEFT JOIN agent_access x ON x.agent_id = a.agent_id\n                    WHERE a.owner_username=%s OR x.principal=%s OR x.principal='root'\n                    ORDER BY a.created_at DESC\"\"\", (user, user))\n            rows = cur.fetchall()\n            conn.close()\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    return {\"agents\": [\n        {\"agent_id\": str(r[0]), \"name\": r[1], \"type\": r[2], \"owner\": r[3], \"harness\": r[4],\n         \"state\": r[5], \"vmid\": r[6], \"node\": r[7], \"ip\": r[8], \"model\": r[9],\n         \"created_at\": r[10].isoformat() if r[10] else None} for r in rows]}\n\n\n@app.post(\"/agents/api/agents\")\nasync def create_agent(request: Request):\n    user, role, err = require(request)\n    if err:\n        return err\n    body = await request.json()\n    name = (body.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n    agent_type = body.get(\"agent_type\", \"STEA\")\n    harness\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}
